{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0185XvHwn4p3mUewS7B5w5jW","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Dataset Conversion Notes\n     2→\n     3→## Overview\n     4→- **Dataset**: Sosa et al. 2025 - \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\"\n     5→- **Date started**: 2026-03-10\n     6→- **Goal**: Convert NWB calcium imaging data to decoder-compatible format\n     7→- **Paper**: Nature Neuroscience, DOI: 10.1038/s41593-025-01985-4\n     8→\n     9→## Step 0: Setup and Initialization\n    10→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    11→\n    12→Python environment: numpy 2.3.5, torch 2.6.0+cu124, CUDA available\n    13→\n    14→Directory contents:\n    15→- `paper.pdf` - Reference paper\n    16→- `methods.txt` - Extracted methods from paper\n    17→- `decoder.py` - Decoder module\n    18→- `train_decoder.py` - Decoder training script\n    19→- `code/` - Reference code (Sosa_et_al_2024 repo: README, notebooks, src/reward_relative/, docs)\n    20→- `data/` - NWB data, 11 subjects (m3, m4, m7, m11-m15, m17-m19), 12-14 sessions each, ~152 total sessions\n    21→\n    22→---\n    23→\n    24→## Step 1: Reference Code Exploration\n    25→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    26→\n    27→### Key Functions Identified\n    28→| Function | File | Stage | Purpose |\n    29→|----------|------|-------|---------|\n    30→| `get_timeseries_data()` | glmUtils.py | LOADING | Extracts trial-aligned behavioral and neural data from sess |\n    31→| `dff()` | preprocessing.py | PROCESSING | Computes dF/F from raw fluorescence: neuropil subtraction, maximin baseline, gaussian smooth |\n    32→| `get_trial_types()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Returns isreward (binary) and morph (env identity) per trial |\n    33→| `get_reward_zones()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Maps scene name to reward zone [start,stop] coords and labels per trial |\n    34→| `define_trial_subsets()` | behavior.py | CURATION | Splits trials into set0/set1 based on switch trial (trial 30) |\n    35→| `calc_place_cells()` | spatial.py | PROCESSING | Identifies place cells via spatial information shuffle test |\n    36→| `multi_anim_sess()` | utilities.py | LOADING | Loads sess pickles, computes dFF, detects PCs, extracts behavior |\n    37→| `dayData` class | dayData.py | PROCESSING | Multi-animal aggregation with speed thresholding, PC detection |\n    38→| `CircularRegression` | decode.py | PROCESSING | Circular-linear regression decoder for position |\n    39→| `train_vs_test_blocks()` | decode.py | PROCESSING | Cross-validated decoder scoring |\n    40→| `get_omission_trials()` | rewardAnalysis.py | LOADING | Gets omission trial indices |\n    41→| `get_omission_inds()` | rewardAnalysis.py | LOADING | Gets rzone entry index on omission trials |\n    42→\n    43→### Key Processing Parameters (from code)\n    44→- **dFF baseline**: maximin filter with 300 sample (~20 sec) window, per trial\n    45→- **dFF formula**: (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n    46→- **Neuropil subtraction**: F_corrected = F - 0.7 * Fneu\n    47→- **dFF smoothing**: Gaussian sigma=2 samples (~0.129 sec)\n    48→- **Deconvolution**: OASIS (from suite2p), tau=0.7\n    49→- **Speed threshold**: 2 cm/s (used in place cell and decoder analysis)\n    50→- **Place cell p-threshold**: 0.05, 100 shuffles, population shuffle method\n    51→- **Interneuron exclusion**: speed correlation > 0.5\n    52→- **Trial alignment**: trial_start_inds to teleport_inds (start-1 to stop-1 in code, 0-indexed)\n    53→- **Position binning**: 10 cm bins, 45 bins for 450 cm track\n    54→- **Track length**: 0-450 cm\n    55→- **Reward zones**: A=[80,130], B=[200,250], C=[320,370] (from reward_zone_dict using X,Y,Z keys)\n    56→- **Switch trial**: trial 30 (0-indexed), change_trial parameter in get_reward_zones\n    57→\n    58→### Key Observation: NWB vs Reference Code Data\n    59→The NWB files contain pre-processed data:\n    60→- `Fluorescence/plane0/data` - raw F (n_timepoints x n_cells)\n    61→- `Neuropil/plane0/data` - neuropil F\n    62→- `Deconvolved/plane0/data` - already deconvolved events\n    63→- `iscell` - suite2p cell classification (2 columns: is_cell, probability)\n    64→- `planeIdx` - plane index for multi-plane recordings (m17, m18)\n    65→- Behavioral timeseries all at imaging frame rate (~15.5 Hz)\n    66→- Scene name embedded in `identifier` field\n    67→\n    68→The NWB files already have the deconvolved events computed. We need to:\n    69→1. Compute dF/F ourselves from F and Fneu (same as reference code)\n    70→2. OR use the pre-computed deconvolved events directly\n    71→3. Filter by iscell\n    72→4. The reference decoder (Fig3) uses deconvolved events, NOT dF/F\n    73→\n    74→### Decoder Analysis from Fig3 notebook\n    75→- Uses `glmUtils.get_timeseries_data()` to get continuous timeseries\n    76→- Neural data: deconvolved events (`sess.timeseries['events']`)\n    77→- Speed threshold: 2 cm/s applied; timepoints with speed < 2 cm/s AND nan licks are excluded\n    78→- Lick sensor error correction: trials with >35% of samples having cumulative lick >2 get licks set to NaN\n    79→- Licks capped at 1 (binary)\n    80→- The decoder predicts circular reward-relative position from neural activity\n    81→- Cross-validation: 10-fold, 90% train, 10% test\n    82→- Uses cell type subsets (rr, track, nonreward_remap) identified via dayData\n    83→\n    84→---\n    85→\n    86→## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n    87→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    88→\n    89→### Data Structure\n    90→NWB files organized as: `data/sub-{mouse}/sub-{mouse}_ses-{session}_behavior+ophys.nwb`\n    91→\n    92→Each NWB file contains:\n    93→- `processing/ophys/Fluorescence/plane{N}/data` - raw fluorescence (n_timepoints x n_ROIs)\n    94→- `processing/ophys/Neuropil/plane{N}/data` - neuropil fluorescence\n    95→- `processing/ophys/Deconvolved/plane{N}/data` - deconvolved events\n    96→- `processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell` - (n_ROIs, 2): [is_cell, probability]\n    97→- `processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/planeIdx` - plane index per ROI\n    98→- `processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/` - position, speed, lick, reward_zone, trial number, trial_start, teleport, environment, scanning, autoreward, Reward (separate timestamps)\n    99→- `general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate` - frame rate\n   100→- `identifier` - contains scene name (e.g., Env1_LocationB_to_A)\n   101→- `general/subject/subject_id` - mouse ID\n   102→- `general/session_id` - session number\n   103→\n   104→Multi-plane animals (m17, m18): Have plane0 and plane1, imaging_rate=31.015625 Hz (15.5 Hz per plane)\n   105→\n   106→### Dataset Size (from data files)\n   107→| Statistic | Value |\n   108→|-----------|-------|\n   109→| Subjects | 11 (m3, m4, m7, m11-m15, m17-m19) |\n   110→| Sessions / subject | m11: 12, all others: 14 |\n   111→| Total sessions | 152 |\n   112→| ROIs/session (example m11 ses-03) | 349 total, 155 iscell=1 |\n   113→| ROIs/session (example m17 ses-03) | 2619 total (1206+1413 planes), 676 iscell=1 |\n   114→| Trials/session (example m11 ses-03) | 80 |\n   115→| Timepoints/session (example m11 ses-03) | 19818 |\n   116→| Frame rate (single plane) | ~15.5 Hz |\n   117→| Frame rate (multi-plane) | ~31 Hz (15.5 Hz per plane) |\n   118→\n   119→---\n   120→\n   121→## Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n   122→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   123→\n   124→### Expected Statistics (from paper/methods)\n   125→| Statistic | Value | Source Quote |\n   126→|-----------|-------|--------------|\n   127→| Total mice (switch) | 11 | \"n = 11 mice\" |\n   128→| Total mice (fixed) | 3 | \"n = 3 mice\" - NOT in our data |\n   129→| Days of imaging | 14 | \"14 days\" (m11 starts day 3, so 12 sessions) |\n   130→| Trials/session | 80.5 +/- 7.4 | \"mean +/- s.d., 80.5 +/- 7.4 trials across 14 mice\" |\n   131→| Neurons/session range | 155-2172 | \"155-2172 putative pyramidal neurons per session\" |\n   132→| Imaging rate | ~15.5 Hz | \"unidirectional scanning at ~15.5 Hz\" |\n   133→| Track length | 450 cm | \"450 cm virtual linear track\" |\n   134→| Reward zone size | 50 cm | \"hidden 50 cm reward zone\" |\n   135→| Zone A | 80-130 cm | \"zone A, 80-130 cm\" |\n   136→| Zone B | 200-250 cm | \"zone B, 200-250 cm\" |\n   137→| Zone C | 320-370 cm | \"zone C, 320-370 cm\" |\n   138→| Reward omission rate | ~15% | \"randomly omitted on ~15% of trials\" |\n   139→| Speed threshold | 2 cm/s | \"excluded activity when moving at <2 cm/s\" |\n   140→| Spatial bins | 45 bins x 10 cm | \"45 bins of 10 cm each\" |\n   141→| Switch trial | 30 | \"set0: 30 trials before switch, set1: after switch\" |\n   142→| Interneurons excluded | 0.42+/-0.85% | \"Pearson corr >0.5 with speed\" |\n   143→| Lick error trials | ~0.65% (~81/12376) | \">30% of samples having cumulative lick >2\" |\n   144→| Teleport zone | ~50 cm + 1-10s jitter | \"gray teleport zone\" |\n   145→\n   146→### Processing Details\n   147→1. **dF/F**: maximin baseline (20s sliding window), per trial, (F-baseline)/|baseline|, Gaussian smooth sigma=2 samples\n   148→2. **Deconvolution**: OASIS algorithm (suite2p), tau=0.7\n   149→3. **Neural data used for decoder**: deconvolved events (not dF/F)\n   150→4. **Speed filtering**: exclude frames with speed < 2 cm/s\n   151→5. **Lick cleaning**: if >35% of samples in trial have cumulative lick >2, set trial licks to NaN; then cap at 1\n   152→6. **Temporal alignment**: all behavioral and neural data at ~15.5 Hz frame rate, already synchronized\n   153→\n   154→### Curation Steps\n   155→\n   156→**Neuron curation rules**:\n   157→1. Suite2p automatic classification + manual curation (stored in iscell)\n   158→2. Interneuron exclusion: Pearson correlation of dF/F with speed > 0.5\n   159→3. For NWB data: iscell column 0 == 1 means cell is included\n   160→\n   161→**Trial curation rules**:\n   162→1. Lick sensor error: trials with >35% of samples having cumulative lick count >2 get licks set to NaN\n   163→2. Only include frames within trial boundaries (trial_start to teleport)\n   164→3. Speed threshold: exclude frames with speed < 2 cm/s\n   165→\n   166→### Decoders Trained (in paper)\n   167→| Decoded variable | Metric | Value |\n   168→|---|---|---|\n   169→| Circular RR position (all PCs) | FDE (fraction decoded error) | 0.10 +/- 0.19 |\n   170→| Circular RR position (RR cells) | Decode score (cosine similarity) | 0.29 +/- 0.11 |\n   171→| Circular RR position (TR cells) | Decode score | 0.32 +/- 0.13 |\n   172→| Circular RR position (non-RR remap) | Decode score | 0.29 +/- 0.11 |\n   173→\n   174→---\n   175→\n   176→## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n   177→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   178→\n   179→### Discrepancies Found\n   180→| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Paper says | Resolution |\n   181→|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n   182→| Reward zone coords | X=[80,130], Y=[200,250], Z=[320,370] | rzone entry ~200 cm for B_to_A session | A=[80-130], B=[200-250], C=[320-370] | Consistent: code maps A->X, B->Y, C->Z |\n   183→| # subjects | 11 switch mice | 11 subject dirs (m3,m4,m7,m11-m15,m17-m19) | 11 switch + 3 fixed | Data has only switch mice, consistent |\n   184→| # sessions for m11 | Starts day 3 | 12 sessions (ses-03 to ses-14) | \"imaging started on day 3 for m11\" | Consistent |\n   185→| Multi-plane | m17, m18 | m17,m18 have plane0+plane1, rate=31Hz | \"m17 and m18 two planes at ~31Hz\" | Consistent |\n   186→| Lick sensor error threshold | >35% samples with lick>2 in code | N/A | \">30% of samples having cumulative lick >2\" | Code uses 35% (>0.35), paper says 30%. Use code value (0.35) |\n   187→| Frame rate | ~15.5 Hz | m11: 15.5078125 Hz | ~15.5 Hz | Consistent |\n   188→| NWB has deconvolved | N/A | Yes, pre-computed | N/A | Can use directly instead of recomputing dF/F then deconvolving |\n   189→| Change trial | default=30, but checks sess.change_reward_trial | Need to verify from NWB | \"first ten post-switch trials\" with autoreward | Use change_trial=30 by default; need to detect from data for robustness |\n   190→\n   191→### Key Consistency Notes\n   192→- The NWB data already has deconvolved events pre-computed via the reference pipeline (suite2p OASIS). We should use these directly.\n   193→- The iscell flags in NWB correspond to the manual curation from suite2p.\n   194→- Reward zone coordinates match between code (using X,Y,Z labels) and paper (A,B,C zones).\n   195→- The scene name is embedded in the NWB `identifier` field and can be parsed to determine reward zone locations.\n   196→- Multi-plane animals have data already interleaved - the behavioral timeseries length matches the fluorescence length.\n   197→\n   198→---\n   199→\n   200→## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n   201→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   202→\n   203→### Variable Mapping\n   204→| Source Variable | Target Field | Transform | Notes |\n   205→|---|---|---|---|\n   206→| Deconvolved events (iscell-filtered) | neural | Per-trial, time-aligned, (n_neurons, n_timepoints) | Use pre-computed deconvolved from NWB |\n   207→| Time from trial start | input[0] | (t - trial_start) / imaging_rate, continuous seconds | Time-varying |\n   208→| Environment (morph) | input[1] | 0=ENV1, 1=ENV2, per-trial scalar | From `environment` behavioral TS |\n   209→| Trial number within session | input[2] | 0-indexed, per-trial scalar | From `trial number` behavioral TS |\n   210→| Previous trial outcome | input[3] | 0=omitted, 1=rewarded, per-trial scalar | From reward detection in previous trial |\n   211→| Distance to reward zone | output[0] | Discretized into 7 bins, time-varying | Compute from position and reward zone coords |\n   212→| Absolute position | output[1] | Discretized into 5 equal bins (90cm each), time-varying | From `position` behavioral TS |\n   213→| Speed | output[2] | Discretized into 5 bins, time-varying | From `speed` behavioral TS |\n   214→| Lick | output[3] | Binary 0/1, time-varying | From `lick` behavioral TS, cap at 1 |\n   215→| Reward zone location | output[4] | 0=A, 1=B, 2=C, per-trial | From scene name + trial number |\n   216→| Reward outcome | output[5] | 0=no, 1=yes, per-trial | From Reward timestamps within trial |\n   217→\n   218→### Processing Pipeline\n   219→1. **Load NWB**: Read all behavioral and neural data\n   220→2. **Filter neurons**: Apply iscell filter; exclude interneurons (speed corr > 0.5)\n   221→3. **Compute dF/F**: From F and Fneu using maximin baseline (needed for interneuron detection)\n   222→4. **Identify trials**: From trial_start and teleport indices\n   223→5. **Determine reward zones**: Parse scene from identifier, use get_reward_zones logic\n   224→6. **For each trial**: Extract neural (deconvolved events) and behavioral data between trial_start and teleport\n   225→7. **No speed filtering for conversion** - the decoder task specifies speed as an output, so we keep all timepoints\n   226→8. **Lick cleaning**: Apply the 35% threshold for lick sensor errors, cap at 1\n   227→9. **Time bin**: Each imaging frame (~64.5 ms) is one time bin\n   228→\n   229→### Key Decisions\n   230→1. **Neural data = deconvolved events**: The paper's decoder uses deconvolved events, and NWB has them pre-computed\n   231→2. **No speed thresholding for time bins**: Speed is an output to decode, so we keep all frames. The reference decoder applied speed thresholding, but our task is different.\n   232→3. **Time bin = imaging frame**: ~64.5 ms per frame at ~15.5 Hz. This matches the native sampling rate.\n   233→4. **Trial alignment = trial start**: As specified in Decoder Task section.\n   234→5. **Interneuron exclusion**: Compute dF/F to get speed-neural correlation, exclude cells with corr > 0.5\n   235→6. **Change trial = 30**: Default from code; determines when reward zone switches within a session\n   236→7. **Reward detection**: Check if any reward timestamp falls within trial window\n   237→\n   238→### Output Discretization\n   239→- **Distance to reward zone** (7 bins): < -50, [-50,-10], [-10,0), 0, (0,10], [10,50], > 50 cm\n   240→  - Distance = position - nearest_reward_zone_edge (signed, negative=before, 0=in zone)\n   241→  - Need to compute: min distance to any point in reward zone (0 if inside)\n   242→- **Absolute position** (5 bins): [0,90), [90,180), [180,270), [270,360), [360,450] cm\n   243→- **Speed** (5 bins): <2, [2,10), [10,20), [20,40), >=40 cm/s\n   244→- **Lick** (2 bins): 0=no, 1=yes\n   245→- **Reward zone location** (3 bins): 0=A, 1=B, 2=C\n   246→- **Reward outcome** (2 bins): 0=no, 1=yes\n   247→\n   248→### Planned Sanity Checks\n   249→- [ ] Verify neuron count per session matches iscell sum\n \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\"\n- **Date started**: 2026-03-10\n- **Goal**: Convert NWB calcium imaging data to decoder-compatible format\n- **Paper**: Nature Neuroscience, DOI: 10.1038/s41593-025-01985-4\n\n## Step 0: Setup and Initialization\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\nPython environment: numpy 2.3.5, torch 2.6.0+cu124, CUDA available\n\nDirectory contents:\n- `paper.pdf` - Reference paper\n- `methods.txt` - Extracted methods from paper\n- `decoder.py` - Decoder module\n- `train_decoder.py` - Decoder training script\n- `code/` - Reference code (Sosa_et_al_2024 repo: README, notebooks, src/reward_relative/, docs)\n- `data/` - NWB data, 11 subjects (m3, m4, m7, m11-m15, m17-m19), 12-14 sessions each, ~152 total sessions\n\n---\n\n## Step 1: Reference Code Exploration\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Key Functions Identified\n| Function | File | Stage | Purpose |\n|----------|------|-------|---------|\n| `get_timeseries_data()` | glmUtils.py | LOADING | Extracts trial-aligned behavioral and neural data from sess |\n| `dff()` | preprocessing.py | PROCESSING | Computes dF/F from raw fluorescence: neuropil subtraction, maximin baseline, gaussian smooth |\n| `get_trial_types()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Returns isreward (binary) and morph (env identity) per trial |\n| `get_reward_zones()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Maps scene name to reward zone [start,stop] coords and labels per trial |\n| `define_trial_subsets()` | behavior.py | CURATION | Splits trials into set0/set1 based on switch trial (trial 30) |\n| `calc_place_cells()` | spatial.py | PROCESSING | Identifies place cells via spatial information shuffle test |\n| `multi_anim_sess()` | utilities.py | LOADING | Loads sess pickles, computes dFF, detects PCs, extracts behavior |\n| `dayData` class | dayData.py | PROCESSING | Multi-animal aggregation with speed thresholding, PC detection |\n| `CircularRegression` | decode.py | PROCESSING | Circular-linear regression decoder for position |\n| `train_vs_test_blocks()` | decode.py | PROCESSING | Cross-validated decoder scoring |\n| `get_omission_trials()` | rewardAnalysis.py | LOADING | Gets omission trial indices |\n| `get_omission_inds()` | rewardAnalysis.py | LOADING | Gets rzone entry index on omission trials |\n\n### Key Processing Parameters (from code)\n- **dFF baseline**: maximin filter with 300 sample (~20 sec) window, per trial\n- **dFF formula**: (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n- **Neuropil subtraction**: F_corrected = F - 0.7 * Fneu\n- **dFF smoothing**: Gaussian sigma=2 samples (~0.129 sec)\n- **Deconvolution**: OASIS (from suite2p), tau=0.7\n- **Speed threshold**: 2 cm/s (used in place cell and decoder analysis)\n- **Place cell p-threshold**: 0.05, 100 shuffles, population shuffle method\n- **Interneuron exclusion**: speed correlation > 0.5\n- **Trial alignment**: trial_start_inds to teleport_inds (start-1 to stop-1 in code, 0-indexed)\n- **Position binning**: 10 cm bins, 45 bins for 450 cm track\n- **Track length**: 0-450 cm\n- **Reward zones**: A=[80,130], B=[200,250], C=[320,370] (from reward_zone_dict using X,Y,Z keys)\n- **Switch trial**: trial 30 (0-indexed), change_trial parameter in get_reward_zones\n\n### Key Observation: NWB vs Reference Code Data\nThe NWB files contain pre-processed data:\n- `Fluorescence/plane0/data` - raw F (n_timepoints x n_cells)\n- `Neuropil/plane0/data` - neuropil F\n- `Deconvolved/plane0/data` - already deconvolved events\n- `iscell` - suite2p cell classification (2 columns: is_cell, probability)\n- `planeIdx` - plane index for multi-plane recordings (m17, m18)\n- Behavioral timeseries all at imaging frame rate (~15.5 Hz)\n- Scene name embedded in `identifier` field\n\nThe NWB files already have the deconvolved events computed. We need to:\n1. Compute dF/F ourselves from F and Fneu (same as reference code)\n2. OR use the pre-computed deconvolved events directly\n3. Filter by iscell\n4. The reference decoder (Fig3) uses deconvolved events, NOT dF/F\n\n### Decoder Analysis from Fig3 notebook\n- Uses `glmUtils.get_timeseries_data()` to get continuous timeseries\n- Neural data: deconvolved events (`sess.timeseries['events']`)\n- Speed threshold: 2 cm/s applied; timepoints with speed < 2 cm/s AND nan licks are excluded\n- Lick sensor error correction: trials with >35% of samples having cumulative lick >2 get licks set to NaN\n- Licks capped at 1 (binary)\n- The decoder predicts circular reward-relative position from neural activity\n- Cross-validation: 10-fold, 90% train, 10% test\n- Uses cell type subsets (rr, track, nonreward_remap) identified via dayData\n\n---\n\n## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Data Structure\nNWB files organized as: `data/sub-{mouse}/sub-{mouse}_ses-{session}_behavior+ophys.nwb`\n\nEach NWB file contains:\n- `processing/ophys/Fluorescence/plane{N}/data` - raw fluorescence (n_timepoints x n_ROIs)\n- `processing/ophys/Neuropil/plane{N}/data` - neuropil fluorescence\n- `processing/ophys/Deconvolved/plane{N}/data` - deconvolved events\n- `processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell` - (n_ROIs, 2): [is_cell, probability]\n- `processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/planeIdx` - plane index per ROI\n- `processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/` - position, speed, lick, reward_zone, trial number, trial_start, teleport, environment, scanning, autoreward, Reward (separate timestamps)\n- `general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate` - frame rate\n- `identifier` - contains scene name (e.g., Env1_LocationB_to_A)\n- `general/subject/subject_id` - mouse ID\n- `general/session_id` - session number\n\nMulti-plane animals (m17, m18): Have plane0 and plane1, imaging_rate=31.015625 Hz (15.5 Hz per plane)\n\n### Dataset Size (from data files)\n| Statistic | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| Subjects | 11 (m3, m4, m7, m11-m15, m17-m19) |\n| Sessions / subject | m11: 12, all others: 14 |\n| Total sessions | 152 |\n| ROIs/session (example m11 ses-03) | 349 total, 155 iscell=1 |\n| ROIs/session (example m17 ses-03) | 2619 total (1206+1413 planes), 676 iscell=1 |\n| Trials/session (example m11 ses-03) | 80 |\n| Timepoints/session (example m11 ses-03) | 19818 |\n| Frame rate (single plane) | ~15.5 Hz |\n| Frame rate (multi-plane) | ~31 Hz (15.5 Hz per plane) |\n\n---\n\n## Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Expected Statistics (from paper/methods)\n| Statistic | Value | Source Quote |\n|-----------|-------|--------------|\n| Total mice (switch) | 11 | \"n = 11 mice\" |\n| Total mice (fixed) | 3 | \"n = 3 mice\" - NOT in our data |\n| Days of imaging | 14 | \"14 days\" (m11 starts day 3, so 12 sessions) |\n| Trials/session | 80.5 +/- 7.4 | \"mean +/- s.d., 80.5 +/- 7.4 trials across 14 mice\" |\n| Neurons/session range | 155-2172 | \"155-2172 putative pyramidal neurons per session\" |\n| Imaging rate | ~15.5 Hz | \"unidirectional scanning at ~15.5 Hz\" |\n| Track length | 450 cm | \"450 cm virtual linear track\" |\n| Reward zone size | 50 cm | \"hidden 50 cm reward zone\" |\n| Zone A | 80-130 cm | \"zone A, 80-130 cm\" |\n| Zone B | 200-250 cm | \"zone B, 200-250 cm\" |\n| Zone C | 320-370 cm | \"zone C, 320-370 cm\" |\n| Reward omission rate | ~15% | \"randomly omitted on ~15% of trials\" |\n| Speed threshold | 2 cm/s | \"excluded activity when moving at <2 cm/s\" |\n| Spatial bins | 45 bins x 10 cm | \"45 bins of 10 cm each\" |\n| Switch trial | 30 | \"set0: 30 trials before switch, set1: after switch\" |\n| Interneurons excluded | 0.42+/-0.85% | \"Pearson corr >0.5 with speed\" |\n| Lick error trials | ~0.65% (~81/12376) | \">30% of samples having cumulative lick >2\" |\n| Teleport zone | ~50 cm + 1-10s jitter | \"gray teleport zone\" |\n\n### Processing Details\n1. **dF/F**: maximin baseline (20s sliding window), per trial, (F-baseline)/|baseline|, Gaussian smooth sigma=2 samples\n2. **Deconvolution**: OASIS algorithm (suite2p), tau=0.7\n3. **Neural data used for decoder**: deconvolved events (not dF/F)\n4. **Speed filtering**: exclude frames with speed < 2 cm/s\n5. **Lick cleaning**: if >35% of samples in trial have cumulative lick >2, set trial licks to NaN; then cap at 1\n6. **Temporal alignment**: all behavioral and neural data at ~15.5 Hz frame rate, already synchronized\n\n### Curation Steps\n\n**Neuron curation rules**:\n1. Suite2p automatic classification + manual curation (stored in iscell)\n2. Interneuron exclusion: Pearson correlation of dF/F with speed > 0.5\n3. For NWB data: iscell column 0 == 1 means cell is included\n\n**Trial curation rules**:\n1. Lick sensor error: trials with >35% of samples having cumulative lick count >2 get licks set to NaN\n2. Only include frames within trial boundaries (trial_start to teleport)\n3. Speed threshold: exclude frames with speed < 2 cm/s\n\n### Decoders Trained (in paper)\n| Decoded variable | Metric | Value |\n|---|---|---|\n| Circular RR position (all PCs) | FDE (fraction decoded error) | 0.10 +/- 0.19 |\n| Circular RR position (RR cells) | Decode score (cosine similarity) | 0.29 +/- 0.11 |\n| Circular RR position (TR cells) | Decode score | 0.32 +/- 0.13 |\n| Circular RR position (non-RR remap) | Decode score | 0.29 +/- 0.11 |\n\n---\n\n## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Discrepancies Found\n| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Paper says | Resolution |\n|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n| Reward zone coords | X=[80,130], Y=[200,250], Z=[320,370] | rzone entry ~200 cm for B_to_A session | A=[80-130], B=[200-250], C=[320-370] | Consistent: code maps A->X, B->Y, C->Z |\n| # subjects | 11 switch mice | 11 subject dirs (m3,m4,m7,m11-m15,m17-m19) | 11 switch + 3 fixed | Data has only switch mice, consistent |\n| # sessions for m11 | Starts day 3 | 12 sessions (ses-03 to ses-14) | \"imaging started on day 3 for m11\" | Consistent |\n| Multi-plane | m17, m18 | m17,m18 have plane0+plane1, rate=31Hz | \"m17 and m18 two planes at ~31Hz\" | Consistent |\n| Lick sensor error threshold | >35% samples with lick>2 in code | N/A | \">30% of samples having cumulative lick >2\" | Code uses 35% (>0.35), paper says 30%. Use code value (0.35) |\n| Frame rate | ~15.5 Hz | m11: 15.5078125 Hz | ~15.5 Hz | Consistent |\n| NWB has deconvolved | N/A | Yes, pre-computed | N/A | Can use directly instead of recomputing dF/F then deconvolving |\n| Change trial | default=30, but checks sess.change_reward_trial | Need to verify from NWB | \"first ten post-switch trials\" with autoreward | Use change_trial=30 by default; need to detect from data for robustness |\n\n### Key Consistency Notes\n- The NWB data already has deconvolved events pre-computed via the reference pipeline (suite2p OASIS). We should use these directly.\n- The iscell flags in NWB correspond to the manual curation from suite2p.\n- Reward zone coordinates match between code (using X,Y,Z labels) and paper (A,B,C zones).\n- The scene name is embedded in the NWB `identifier` field and can be parsed to determine reward zone locations.\n- Multi-plane animals have data already interleaved - the behavioral timeseries length matches the fluorescence length.\n\n---\n\n## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Variable Mapping\n| Source Variable | Target Field | Transform | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Deconvolved events (iscell-filtered) | neural | Per-trial, time-aligned, (n_neurons, n_timepoints) | Use pre-computed deconvolved from NWB |\n| Time from trial start | input[0] | (t - trial_start) / imaging_rate, continuous seconds | Time-varying |\n| Environment (morph) | input[1] | 0=ENV1, 1=ENV2, per-trial scalar | From `environment` behavioral TS |\n| Trial number within session | input[2] | 0-indexed, per-trial scalar | From `trial number` behavioral TS |\n| Previous trial outcome | input[3] | 0=omitted, 1=rewarded, per-trial scalar | From reward detection in previous trial |\n| Distance to reward zone | output[0] | Discretized into 7 bins, time-varying | Compute from position and reward zone coords |\n| Absolute position | output[1] | Discretized into 5 equal bins (90cm each), time-varying | From `position` behavioral TS |\n| Speed | output[2] | Discretized into 5 bins, time-varying | From `speed` behavioral TS |\n| Lick | output[3] | Binary 0/1, time-varying | From `lick` behavioral TS, cap at 1 |\n| Reward zone location | output[4] | 0=A, 1=B, 2=C, per-trial | From scene name + trial number |\n| Reward outcome | output[5] | 0=no, 1=yes, per-trial | From Reward timestamps within trial |\n\n### Processing Pipeline\n1. **Load NWB**: Read all behavioral and neural data\n2. **Filter neurons**: Apply iscell filter; exclude interneurons (speed corr > 0.5)\n3. **Compute dF/F**: From F and Fneu using maximin baseline (needed for interneuron detection)\n4. **Identify trials**: From trial_start and teleport indices\n5. **Determine reward zones**: Parse scene from identifier, use get_reward_zones logic\n6. **For each trial**: Extract neural (deconvolved events) and behavioral data between trial_start and teleport\n7. **No speed filtering for conversion** - the decoder task specifies speed as an output, so we keep all timepoints\n8. **Lick cleaning**: Apply the 35% threshold for lick sensor errors, cap at 1\n9. **Time bin**: Each imaging frame (~64.5 ms) is one time bin\n\n### Key Decisions\n1. **Neural data = deconvolved events**: The paper's decoder uses deconvolved events, and NWB has them pre-computed\n2. **No speed thresholding for time bins**: Speed is an output to decode, so we keep all frames. The reference decoder applied speed thresholding, but our task is different.\n3. **Time bin = imaging frame**: ~64.5 ms per frame at ~15.5 Hz. This matches the native sampling rate.\n4. **Trial alignment = trial start**: As specified in Decoder Task section.\n5. **Interneuron exclusion**: Compute dF/F to get speed-neural correlation, exclude cells with corr > 0.5\n6. **Change trial = 30**: Default from code; determines when reward zone switches within a session\n7. **Reward detection**: Check if any reward timestamp falls within trial window\n\n### Output Discretization\n- **Distance to reward zone** (7 bins): < -50, [-50,-10], [-10,0), 0, (0,10], [10,50], > 50 cm\n  - Distance = position - nearest_reward_zone_edge (signed, negative=before, 0=in zone)\n  - Need to compute: min distance to any point in reward zone (0 if inside)\n- **Absolute position** (5 bins): [0,90), [90,180), [180,270), [270,360), [360,450] cm\n- **Speed** (5 bins): <2, [2,10), [10,20), [20,40), >=40 cm/s\n- **Lick** (2 bins): 0=no, 1=yes\n- **Reward zone location** (3 bins): 0=A, 1=B, 2=C\n- **Reward outcome** (2 bins): 0=no, 1=yes\n\n### Planned Sanity Checks\n- [ ] Verify neuron count per session matches iscell sum\n- [ ] Verify trial count matches reference statistics (~80.5 mean)\n- [ ] Verify position ranges are [0, 450] within trials\n- [ ] Verify reward zone entry positions match expected coordinates\n- [ ] Verify ~15% omission rate\n- [ ] Verify ~85% reward rate\n- [ ] Compare total sessions (152) and subjects (11)\n- [ ] Spot-check deconvolved event values match NWB directly\n\n---\n\n## Step 6: Script Development\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\nImplemented `convert_data.py` with:\n- NWB file loading using h5py\n- Scene name  113→        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n   114→        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'A'\n   115→    elif 'B_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'C':\n   116→ rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'C'\n   120→    elif 'C_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'B':\n   121→        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n   122→        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'C'\n   123→        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n   124→        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'B'\n   125→    elif 'Training' in scene:\n   126→        rz_coords[:] = [275, 325]\n   127→        rz_labels[:] = 'T'\n   128→    else:\n   129→        print(f\"  WARNING: Unrecognized scene '{scene}', defaulting to zone A\")\n   130→        rz_coords[:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n   131→        rz_labels[:] = 'A'\n   132→\n   133→    return rz_coords, rz_labels\n   134→\n   135→\n   136→def compute_dff_trial(F_trial, Fneu_trial, baseline_window=BASELINE_WINDOW):\n   137→    \"\"\"Compute dF/F for a single trial using maximin baseline.\n   138→    Mirrors preprocessing.dff() from reference code.\n   139→\n   140→    Args:\n   141→        F_trial: (n_cells, n_timepoints) raw fluorescence for one trial\n   142→        Fneu_trial: (n_cells, n_timepoints) neuropil fluorescence\n   143→    Returns:\n   144→        dff: (n_cells, n_timepoints) dF/F\n   145→    \"\"\"\n   146→    # Neuropil subtraction\n   147→    F_corr = F_trial - NEUROPIL_COEF * Fneu_trial\n   148→\n   149→    n_cells, n_t = F_corr.shape\n   150→    if n_t < 3:\n   151→        return np.zeros_like(F_corr)\n   152→\n   153→    # Maximin baseline per cell\n   154→    # 1. Gaussian smooth\n   155→    smoothed = gaussian_filter1d(F_corr, sigma=15, axis=1)\n   156→    # 2. Minimum filter\n   157→    baseline = minimum_filter1d(smoothed, size=min(baseline_window, n_t), axis=1)\n   158→    # 3. Maximum filter (morphological dilation)\n   159→    baseline = maximum_filter1d(baseline, size=min(baseline_window, n_t), axis=1)\n   160→\n   161→    # dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n   162→    abs_baseline = np.abs(baseline)\n   163→    abs_baseline[abs_baseline < 1e-10] = 1e-10  # avoid division by zero\n   164→    dff = (F_corr - baseline) / abs_baseline\n   165→\n   166→    # Smooth dF/F\n   167→    dff = gaussian_filter1d(dff, sigma=DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n   168→\n   169→    return dff\n   170→\n   171→\n   172→def detect_interneurons(dff_all, speed_all, threshold=INTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD):\n   173→    \"\"\"Detect putative interneurons by correlation of dF/F with speed.\n   174→    Vectorized implementation for speed.\n   175→    Args:\n   176→        dff_all: (n_cells, n_total_timepoints) - dF/F for all timepoints in session\n   177→        speed_all: (n_total_timepoints,) - speed for all timepoints\n   178→    Returns:\n   179→        is_interneuron: (n_cells,) boolean mask\n   180→    \"\"\"\n   181→    n_cells = dff_all.shape[0]\n   182→    is_interneuron = np.zeros(n_cells, dtype=bool)\n   183→\n   184→    # Only use valid (non-NaN) timepoints\n   185→    valid = ~np.isnan(speed_all) & ~np.isnan(dff_all[0])\n   186→    if valid.sum() < 10:\n   187→        return is_interneuron\n   188→\n   189→    speed_valid = speed_all[valid].astype(np.float64)\n   190→    dff_valid = dff_all[:, valid].astype(np.float64)  # (n_cells, n_valid)\n   191→\n   192→    # Vectorized Pearson correlation\n   193→    speed_mean = speed_valid.mean()\n   194→    speed_centered = speed_valid - speed_mean\n   195→    speed_std = np.sqrt(np.sum(speed_centered ** 2))\n   196→\n   197→    if speed_std < 1e-10:\n   198→        return is_interneuron\n   199→\n   200→    dff_mean = dff_valid.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n   201→    dff_centered = dff_valid - dff_mean\n   202→    dff_std = np.sqrt(np.sum(dff_centered ** 2, axis=1))\n   203→\n   204→    # Correlation = dot(dff_centered, speed_centered) / (dff_std * speed_std)\n   205→    corr = dff_centered @ speed_centered / (dff_std * speed_std + 1e-10)\n   206→\n   207→    is_interneuron = corr > threshold\n   208→\n   209→    return is_interneuron\n   210→\n   211→\n   212→def compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\n   213→    \"\"\"Compute signed distance from position to nearest point in reward zone.\n   214→    Negative = before reward zone, 0 = inside, positive = after.\n   215→    \"\"\"\n   216→    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n   217→    before = position < rz_start\n   218→    inside = (position >= rz_start) & (position <= rz_end)\n   219→    after = position > rz_end\n   220→    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start  # negative\n   221→    dist[inside] = 0.0\n   222→    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end  # positive\n   223→    return dist\n   224→\n   225→\n   226→def discretize_distance_to_rz(dist):\n   227→    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins.\n   228→    0: < -50 cm\n   229→    1: -50 to -10 cm\n   230→    2: -10 cm to < 0 cm\n   231→    3: 0 cm (inside reward zone)\n   232→    4: >0 cm to +10 cm\n   233→    5: +10 to +50 cm\n   234→    6: > +50 cm\n   235→    \"\"\"\n   236→    out = np.zeros(len(dist), dtype=np.int64)\n   237→    out[dist < -50] = 0\n   238→    out[(dist >= -50) & (dist < -10)] = 1\n   239→    out[(dist >= -10) & (dist < 0)] = 2\n   240→    out[dist == 0] = 3\n   241→    out[(dist > 0) & (dist <= 10)] = 4\n   242→    out[(dist > 10) & (dist <= 50)] = 5\n   243→    out[dist > 50] = 6\n   244→    return out\n   245→\n   246→\n   247→def discretize_position(position, n_bins=POSITION_BINS):\n   248→    \"\"\"Discretize position into equal-sized bins.\"\"\"\n   249→    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, TRACK_LENGTH, n_bins + 1)\n   250→    binned = np.digitize(position, bin_edges) - 1\n   251→    binned = np.clip(binned, 0, n_bins - 1)\n   252→    return binned\n   253→\n   254→\n   255→def discretize_speed(speed):\n   256→    \"\"\"Discretize speed into 5 bins.\n   257→    0: < 2 cm/s\n   258→    1: 2-10 cm/s\n   259→    2: 10-20 cm/s\n   260→    3: 20-40 cm/s\n   261→    4: > 40 cm/s\n   262→    \"\"\"\n   263→    out = np.zeros(len(speed), dtype=np.int64)\n   264→    out[speed < 2] = 0\n   265→    out[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\n   266→    out[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\n   267→    out[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\n   268→    out[speed >= 40] = 4\n   269→    return out\n   270→\n   271→\n   272→def rz_label_to_idx(label):\n   273→    \"\"\"Convert reward zone label to index: A=0, B=1, C=2.\"\"\"\n   274→    mapping = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}\n   275→    return mapping.get(label, -1)\n   276→\n   277→\n   278→def detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time):\n   279→    \"\"\"Check if any reward was delivered during the trial.\"\"\"\n   280→    if len(reward_timestamps) == 0:\n   281→        return False\n   282→    return np.any((reward_timestamps >= trial_start_time) & (reward_timestamps <= trial_end_time))\n   283→\n   284→\n   285→def process_session(nwb_path, show_processing=False, session_label=\"\"):\n   286→    \"\"\"Process a single NWB file and return session data.\n   287→\n   288→    Returns dict with keys: neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n   289→        subject_id, n_neurons, brain_region, session_info, or None if invalid.\n   290→    \"\"\"\n   291→    t0 = time.time()\n   292→\n   293→    with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n   294→        # --- Metadata ---\n   295→        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode()\n   296→        session_id = f['general/session_id'][()].decode()\n   297→        identifier = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n   298→        imaging_rate = f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate'][()]\n   299→        scene = get_scene_from_identifier(identifier)\n   300→\n   301→        bts = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries']\n   302→\n   303→        # --- Behavioral data ---\n   304→        position = bts['position/data'][:]\n   305→        speed = bts['speed/data'][:]\n   306→        lick_raw = bts['lick/data'][:]\n   307→        rzone_cumul = bts['reward_zone/data'][:]\n   308→        trial_num = bts['trial number/data'][:]\n   309→        trial_start_flag = bts['trial_start/data'][:]\n   310→        teleport_flag = bts['teleport/data'][:]\n   311→        environment = bts['environment/data'][:]\n   312→        scanning = bts['scanning/data'][:]\n   313→        pos_timestamps = bts['position/timestamps'][:]\n   314→\n   315→        # Reward: stored with separate timestamps\n   316→        reward_timestamps = bts['Reward/timestamps'][:]\n   317→        reward_data = bts['Reward/data'][:]\n   318→\n   319→        # --- Neural data ---\n   320→        ophys = f['processing/ophys']\n   321→        iscell = ophys['ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell'][:]\n   322→        plane_idx = ophys['ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/planeIdx'][:]\n   323→\n   324→        # Get fluorescence and deconvolved data from all planes\n   325→        fluor_planes = sorted([k for k in ophys['Fluorescence'].keys() if k.startswith('plane')])\n   326→\n   327→        # Load deconvolved events (already computed in NWB)\n   328→        deconv_list = []\n   329→        F_list = []\n   330→        Fneu_list = []\n   331→        plane_assignment = []\n   332→\n   333→        for pi, plane_name in enumerate(fluor_planes):\n   334→            deconv_data = ophys[f'Deconvolved/{plane_name}/data'][:]  # (n_timepoints, n_rois_plane)\n   335→            F_data = ophys[f'Fluorescence/{plane_name}/data'][:]\n   336→            Fneu_data = ophys[f'Neuropil/{plane_name}/data'][:]\n   337→            deconv_list.append(deconv_data)\n   338→            F_list.append(F_data)\n   339→            Fneu_list.append(Fneu_data)\n   340→\n   341→        # Concatenate across planes: (n_timepoints, n_total_rois)\n   342→        deconv_all = np.concatenate(deconv_list, axis=1)\n   343→        F_all = np.concatenate(F_list, axis=1)\n   344→        Fneu_all = np.concatenate(Fneu_list, axis=1)\n   345→\n   346→    # Align lengths: neural and behavioral may differ by 1 frame in multi-plane recordings\n   347→    n_timepoints_neural = deconv_all.shape[0]\n   348→    n_timepoints_behav = len(position)\n   349→    n_timepoints_total = min(n_timepoints_neural, n_timepoints_behav)\n   350→    if n_timepoints_neural != n_timepoints_behav:\n   351→        deconv_all = deconv_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n   352→        F_all = F_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n   353→        Fneu_all = Fneu_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n   354→        position = position[:n_timepoints_total]\n   355→        speed = speed[:n_timepoints_total]\n   356→        lick_raw = lick_raw[:n_timepoints_total]\n   357→        rzone_cumul = rzone_cumul[:n_timepoints_total]\n   358→        trial_num = trial_num[:n_timepoints_total]\n   359→        trial_start_flag = trial_start_flag[:n_timepoints_total]\n   360→        teleport_flag = teleport_flag[:n_timepoints_total]\n   361→        environment = environment[:n_timepoints_total]\n   362→        scanning = scanning[:n_timepoints_total]\n   363→        pos_timestamps = pos_timestamps[:n_timepoints_total]\n   364→\n   365→    n_rois_total = deconv_all.shape[1]\n   366→\n   367→    # For multi-plane, effective rate per plane is imaging_rate / n_planes\n   368→    n_planes = len(fluor_planes)\n   369→    effective_rate = imaging_rate if n_planes == 1 else imaging_rate / n_planes\n   370→    time_bin_ms = 1000.0 / effective_rate\n   371→\n   372→    # --- Filter by iscell ---\n   373→    cell_mask = iscell[:, 0].astype(bool)\n   374→    n_cells_iscell = cell_mask.sum()\n   375→\n   376→    if n_cells_iscell < 2:\n   377→        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_cells_iscell} cells after iscell filter\")\n   378→        return None\n   379→\n   380→    # --- Find trial boundaries ---\n   381→    # trial_start_flag and teleport_flag are binary indicators\n   382→    trial_start_inds = np.where(trial_start_flag > 0)[0]\n   383→    teleport_inds = np.where(teleport_flag > 0)[0]\n   384→\n   385→    n_trials = min(len(trial_start_inds), len(teleport_inds))\n   386→    if n_trials < 2:\n   387→        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_trials} trials\")\n   388→        return None\n   389→\n   390→    trial_start_inds = trial_start_inds[:n_trials]\n   391→    teleport_inds = teleport_inds[:n_trials]\n   392→\n   393→    # --- Get reward zones ---\n   394→    rz_coords, rz_labels = get_reward_zones(scene, n_trials)\n   395→\n   396→    # --- Compute dF/F for interneuron detection ---\n   397→    # We compute dF/F per trial, then concatenate for speed correlation\n   398→    F_cells = F_all[:, cell_mask].T  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n   399→    Fneu_cells = Fneu_all[:, cell_mask].T\n   400→    deconv_cells = deconv_all[:, cell_mask].T  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n   401→\n   402→    # Compute dF/F per trial for interneuron detection\n   403→    dff_full = np.full_like(F_cells, np.nan)\n   404→    for i in range(n_trials):\n   405→        s = trial_start_inds[i]\n   406→        e = teleport_inds[i]\n   407→        if e <= s:\n   408→            continue\n   409→        dff_full[:, s:e] = compute_dff_trial(F_cells[:, s:e], Fneu_cells[:, s:e])\n   410→\n   411→    # Detect interneurons\n   412→    is_interneuron = detect_interneurons(dff_full, speed)\n   413→    n_interneurons = is_interneuron.sum()\n   414→\n   415→    # Final cell mask: iscell AND not interneuron\n   416→    final_cell_mask = ~is_interneuron\n   417→    n_final_cells = final_cell_mask.sum()\n   418→\n   419→    if n_final_cells < 2:\n   420→        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_final_cells} cells after interneuron exclusion\")\n   421→        return None\n   422→\n   423→    # Apply final cell filter to deconvolved events\n   424→    neural_data = deconv_cells[final_cell_mask]  # (n_final_cells, n_timepoints)\n   425→\n   426→    # --- Lick processing ---\n   427→    lick = lick_raw.copy()\n   428→\n   429→    # --- Process trials ---\n   430→    neural_trials = []\n   431→    input_trials = []\n   432→    output_trials = []\n   433→    valid_trial_count = 0\n   434→\n   435→    prev_trial_rewarded = 0  # For the first trial, assume no previous reward\n   436→\n   437→    for i in range(n_trials):\n   438→        s = trial_start_inds[i]\n   439→        e = teleport_inds[i]\n   440→\n   441→        if e <= s or (e - s) < 2:\n   442→            # Still need to track prev_trial_rewarded\n   443→            trial_start_time = pos_timestamps[s] if s < len(pos_timestamps) else 0\n   444→            trial_end_time = pos_timestamps[min(e, len(pos_timestamps) - 1)]\n   445→            was_rewarded = detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time)\n   446→            prev_trial_rewarded = int(was_rewarded)\n   447→            continue\n   448→\n   449→        # Extract data for this trial\n   450→        trial_pos = position[s:e]\n   451→        trial_speed = speed[s:e]\n   452→        trial_lick = lick[s:e].copy()\n   453→        trial_neural = neural_data[:, s:e]  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n   454→        trial_env = environment[s:e]\n   455→        trial_timestamps = pos_timestamps[s:e]\n   456→\n   457→        n_t = e - s\n   458→\n   459→        # Lick sensor error correction (from reference code):\n   460→        # if >35% of samples have cumulative lick count > 2, set licks to NaN for this trial\n   461→        if np.sum(trial_lick > 2) / n_t > LICK_ERROR_FRACTION_THRESHOLD:\n   462→            trial_lick[:] = 0  # set to 0 instead of NaN for decoder output\n   463→        # Cap licks at 1 (binary)\n   464→        trial_lick[trial_lick > 1] = 1\n   465→        trial_lick = (trial_lick > 0).astype(np.float32)\n   466→\n   467→        # --- Determine reward for this trial ---\n   468→        trial_start_time = trial_timestamps[0]\n   469→        trial_end_time = trial_timestamps[-1]\n   470→        was_rewarded = detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time)\n   471→\n   472→        # --- Inputs ---\n   473→        # Time from start of trial in seconds\n   474→        time_from_start = (np.arange(n_t) / effective_rate).astype(np.float32)\n   475→\n   476→        # Environment type (per trial scalar)\n   477→        env_val = np.median(trial_env[trial_env >= 0]) if np.any(trial_env >= 0) else 0.0\n   478→        env_type = np.float32(env_val)\n   479→\n   480→        # Trial number (per trial scalar)\n   481→        trial_number = np.float32(i)\n   482→\n   483→        # Previous trial outcome (per trial scalar)\n   484→        prev_outcome = np.float32(prev_trial_rewarded)\n   485→\n   486→        # Build input array: time_from_start is (1, n_t), others are (1,) scalars\n   487→        input_data = np.array([\n   488→            time_from_start,                     # (n_t,) time-varying\n   489→        ], dtype=np.float32)  # shape (1, n_t)\n   490→        # Add per-trial scalars\n   491→        trial_scalars = np.array([env_type, trial_number, prev_outcome], dtype=np.float32)\n   492→        # Combine: first row is time-varying, then per-trial scalars as separate 1D arrays\n   493→        # Per spec: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,) for per-trial\n   494→        # We'll put time-varying first, then per-trial scalars as single values\n   495→        # Actually, the format allows mixed: time-varying have shape (n_input_tv, n_t)\n   496→        # and per-trial have shape (n_input_pt,)\n   497→        # But the spec says input_data shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,)\n   498→        # Let's make time-varying inputs as (1, n_t) and per-trial as (3,)\n   499→        # Actually re-reading the spec: it's a single array per trial\n   500→        # Best approach: stack all as (n_input, n_timepoints) where per-trial values are repeated\n   501→        input_arr = np.zeros((4, n_t), dtype=np.float32)\n   502→        input_arr[0, :] = time_from_start\n   503→        input_arr[1, :] = env_type  # broadcast\n   504→        input_arr[2, :] = trial_number  # broadcast\n   505→        input_arr[3, :] = prev_outcome  # broadcast\n   506→\n   507→        # --- Outputs ---\n   508→        # Distance to reward zone\n   509→        rz_start, rz_end = rz_coords[i]\n   510→        dist_to_rz = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, rz_start, rz_end)\n   511→        dist_to_rz_binned = discretize_distance_to_rz(dist_to_rz)\n   512→\n   513→        # Absolute position\n   514→        pos_binned = discretize_position(trial_pos)\n   515→\n   516→        # Speed\n   517→        speed_binned = discretize_speed(trial_speed)\n   518→\n   519→        # Lick (already binary)\n   520→        lick_binned = trial_lick.astype(np.int64)\n   521→\n   522→        # Reward zone location (per-trial)\n   523→        rz_loc = rz_label_to_idx(rz_labels[i])\n   524→\n   525→        # Reward outcome (per-trial)\n   526→        reward_outcome = int(was_rewarded)\n   527→\n   528→        # Build output array: time-varying outputs + per-trial\n   529→        # Time-varying: (4, n_t), per-trial: (2,)\n   530→        # Again, make everything (n_output, n_t) with per-trial broadcast\n   531→        output_arr = np.zeros((6, n_t), dtype=np.int64)\n   532→        output_arr[0, :] = dist_to_rz_binned\n   533→        output_arr[1, :] = pos_binned\n   534→        output_arr[2, :] = speed_binned\n   535→        output_arr[3, :] = lick_binned\n   536→        output_arr[4, :] = rz_loc  # broadcast\n   537→        output_arr[5, :] = reward_outcome  # broadcast\n   538→\n   539→        # Store\n   540→        neural_trials.append(trial_neural.astype(np.float32))\n   541→        input_trials.append(input_arr)\n   542→        output_trials.append(output_arr)\n   543→\n   544→        # Update previous trial reward\n   545→        prev_trial_rewarded = int(was_rewarded)\n   546→        valid_trial_count += 1\n   547→\n   548→    if valid_trial_count < 2:\n   549→        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {valid_trial_count} valid trials\")\n   550→        return None\n   551→\n   552→    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n   553→    print(f\"  {session_label}: {n_final_cells} neurons, {valid_trial_count} trials, \"\n   554→          f\"{n_interneurons} interneurons removed, {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n   555→\n   556→    # --- Plotting ---\n   557→    if show_processing and valid_trial_count > 0:\n   558→        plot_processing(\n   559→            neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n   560→            position, speed, lick, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds,\n   561→            rz_coords, rz_labels, reward_timestamps, pos_timestamps,\n   562→            effective_rate, session_label, n_trials\n   563→        )\n   564→\n   565→    return {\n   566→        'neural_trials': neural_trials,\n   567→        'input_trials': input_trials,\n   568→        'output_trials': output_trials,\n   569→        'subject_id': subject_id,\n   570→        'n_neurons': n_final_cells,\n   571→        'brain_region': 'CA1',\n   572→        'n_interneurons': n_interneurons,\n   573→        'n_iscell': n_cells_iscell,\n   574→        'session_info': ============================================================\n# Constants from reference code/paper\n# ============================================================\nTRACK_LENGTH = 450.0  # cm\nREWARD_ZONE_DICT = {\n    'A': [80, 130],   # = 'X' in code\n    'B': [200, 250],  # = 'Y' in code\n    'C': [320, 370],  # = 'Z' in code\n}\nDEFAULT_CHANGE_TRIAL = 30  # 0-indexed trial where reward zone switches\nINTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD = 0.5\nLICK_ERROR_FRACTION_THRESHOLD = 0.35  # from reference code (>35% samples with lick>2)\nNEUROPIL_COEF = 0.7\nDFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 2  # samples\nBASELINE_WINDOW = 300  # samples (~20 sec at 15.5 Hz)\n\n# Position bins for output discretization\nPOSITION_BINS = 5  # equal-size bins over [0, 450]\nPOSITION_BIN_EDGES = np.linspace(0, TRACK_LENGTH, POSITION_BINS + 1)\n\n# Speed bins\nSPEED_BIN_EDGES = [0, 2, 10, 20, 40, np.inf]\n\n# Distance to reward zone bins\nDIST_RZ_BIN_EDGES = [-np.inf, -50, -10, 0, 0, 10, 50, np.inf]\n# Special handling: bin 3 is exactly 0 (inside reward zone)\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert NWB data to decoder format')\n parser.parse_args()\n\n\ndef get_scene_from_identifier(identifier):\n    \"\"\"Extract scene name from NWB identifier field.\n    e.g. '/data/InVivoDA/GCAMP11/23_02_2023/Env1_LocationB_to_A' -> 'Env1_LocationB_to_A'\n    \"\"\"\n    return identifier.split('/')[-1]\n\n\ndef get_reward_zones(scene, n_trials, change_trial=DEFAULT_CHANGE_TRIAL):\n    \"\"\"Map scene name to reward zone coordinates per trial.\n    Mirrors behavior.get_reward_zones() from reference code.\n    The reference code checks for 'X_to' in scene and scene[-1] to handle\n    both within-env (Env1_LocationA_to_B) and cross-env (Env1_A_to_Env2_B) switches.\n\n    Returns:\n        rz_coords: (n_trials, 2) array of [start, stop] positions\n        rz_labels: (n_trials,) array of 'A', 'B', or 'C' labels\n    \"\"\"\n    rz_coords = np.zeros((n_trials, 2))\n    rz_labels = np.empty(n_trials, dtype='U1')\n\n    # Single-location sessions (no switch)\n    if scene.endswith('LocationA') or (scene.endswith('_A') and '_to' not in scene.lower()):\n        # Matches Env1_LocationA, Env2_LocationA\n        # But NOT Env1_LocationB_to_A (has '_to')\n        pass  # fall through to switch check below\n\n    # Use same logic as reference code: check 'X_to' and last character\n    if 'Location' in scene and '_to' not in scene:\n        # Single location: Env1_LocationA, Env2_LocationB, etc.\n        loc = scene.split('Location')[-1]  # 'A', 'B', or 'C'\n        rz_coords[:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[loc]\n        rz_labels[:] = loc\n    elif 'A_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'B':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'A'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'B'\n    elif 'B_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'A':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'B'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'C'\n    elif 'C_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'A':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'C'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'A'\n    elif 'B_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'C':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'B'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'C'\n    elif 'C_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'B':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'C'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n baseline_window=BASELINE_WINDOW):\n    \"\"\"Compute dF/F for a single trial using maximin baseline.\n    Mirrors preprocessing.dff() from reference code.\n\n    Args:\n        F_trial: (n_cells, n_timepoints) raw fluorescence for one trial\n        Fneu_trial: (n_cells, n_timepoints) neuropil fluorescence\n    Returns:\n        dff: (n_cells, n_timepoints) dF/F\n    \"\"\"\n    # Neuropil subtraction\n    F_corr = F_trial - NEUROPIL_COEF * Fneu_trial\n\n    n_cells, n_t = F_corr.shape\n    if n_t < 3:\n        return np.zeros_like(F_corr)\n\n    # Maximin baseline per cell\n    # 1. Gaussian smooth\n    smoothed = gaussian_filter1d(F_corr, sigma=15, axis=1)\n    # 2. Minimum filter\n    baseline = minimum_filter1d(smoothed, size=min(baseline_window, n_t), axis=1)\n    # 3. Maximum filter (morphological dilation)\n    baseline = maximum_filter1d(baseline, size=min(baseline_window, n_t), axis=1)\n\n    # dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n    abs_baseline = np.abs(baseline)\n    abs_baseline[abs_baseline < 1e-10] = 1e-10  # avoid division by zero\n    dff = (F_corr - baseline) / abs_baseline\n\n    # Smooth dF/F\n    dff = gaussian_filter1d(dff, sigma=DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n\n    return dff\n\n\ndef detect_interneurons(dff_all, speed_all, threshold=INTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD):\n    \"\"\"Detect putative interneurons by correlation of dF/F with speed.\n    Vectorized implementation for speed.\n    Args:\n        dff_all: (n_cells, n_total_timepoints) - dF/F for all timepoints in session\n        speed_all: (n_total_timepoints,) - speed for all timepoints\n    Returns:\n        is_interneuron: (n_cells,) boolean mask\n    \"\"\"\n    n_cells = dff_all.shape[0]\n    is_interneuron = np.zeros(n_cells, dtype=bool)\n\n    # Only use valid (non-NaN) timepoints\n    valid = ~np.isnan(speed_all) & ~np.isnan(dff_all[0])\n    if valid.sum() < 10:\n        return is_interneuron\n\n    speed_valid = speed_all[valid].astype(np.float64)\n    dff_valid = dff_all[:, valid].astype(np.float64)  # (n_cells, n_valid)\n\n    # Vectorized Pearson correlation\n    speed_mean = speed_valid.mean()\n    speed_centered = speed_valid - speed_mean\n    speed_std = np.sqrt(np.sum(speed_centered ** 2))\n\n    if speed_std < 1e-10:\n        return is_interneuron\n\n    dff_mean = dff_valid.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    dff_centered = dff_valid - dff_mean\n    dff_std = np.sqrt(np.sum(dff_centered ** 2, axis=1))\n\n    # Correlation = dot(dff_centered, speed_centered) / (dff_std * speed_std)\n    corr = dff_centered @ speed_centered / (dff_std * speed_std + 1e-10)\n\n    is_interneuron = corr > threshold\n\n    return is_interneuron\n\n\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\n    \"\"\"Compute signed distance from position to nearest point in reward zone.\n    Negative = before reward zone, 0 = inside, positive = after.\n    \"\"\"\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n    before = position < rz_start\n    inside = (position >= rz_start) & (position <= rz_end)\n    after = position > rz_end\n    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start  # negative\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\n    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end  # positive\n    return dist\n\n\ndef discretize_distance_to_rz(dist):\n    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins.\n    0: < -50 cm\n    1: -50 to -10 cm\n    2: -10 cm to < 0 cm\n    3: 0 cm (inside reward zone)\n    4: >0 cm to +10 cm\n    5: +10 to +50 cm\n    6: > +50 cm\n    \"\"\"\n    out = np.zeros(len(dist), dtype=np.int64)\n    out[dist < -50] = 0\n    out[(dist >= -50) & (dist < -10)] = 1\n    out[(dist >= -10) & (dist < 0)] = 2\n    out[dist == 0] = 3\n    out[(dist > 0) & (dist <= 10)] = 4\n    out[(dist > 10) & (dist <= 50)] = 5\n    out[dist > 50] = 6\n    return out\n\n\ndef discretize_position(position, n_bins=POSITION_BINS):\n    \"\"\"Discretize position into equal-sized bins.\"\"\"\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, TRACK_LENGTH, n_bins + 1)\n    binned = np.digitize(position, bin_edges) - 1\n    binned = np.clip(binned, 0, n_bins - 1)\n    return binned\n\n\ndef discretize_speed(speed):\n    \"\"\"Discretize speed into 5 bins.\n    0: < 2 cm/s\n    1: 2-10 cm/s\n    2: 10-20 cm/s\n    3: 20-40 cm/s\n    4: > 40 cm/s\n    \"\"\"\n    out = np.zeros(len(speed), dtype=np.int64)\n    out[speed < 2] = 0\n    out[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\n    out[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\n    out[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\n    out[speed >= 40] = 4\n    return out\n\n\ndef rz_label_to_idx(label):\n    \"\"\"Convert reward zone label to index: A=0, B=1, C=2.\"\"\"\n    mapping = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}\n    return mapping.get(label, -1)\n\n\ndef detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time):\n    \"\"\"Check if any reward was delivered during the trial.\"\"\"\n    if len(reward_timestamps) == 0:\n        return False\n    return np.any((reward_timestamps >= trial_start_time) & (reward_timestamps <= trial_end_time))\n\n\ndef process_session(nwb_path, show_processing=False, session_label=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Process a single NWB file and return session data.\n\n    Returns dict with keys: neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n        subject_id, n_neurons, brain_region, session_info, or None if invalid.\n    \"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n        # --- Metadata ---\n        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode()\n        session_id = f['general/session_id'][()].decode()\n        identifier = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n        imaging_rate = f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate'][()]\n        scene = get_scene_from_identifier(identifier)\n\n        bts = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries']\n\n        # --- Behavioral data ---\n        position = bts['position/data'][:]\n        speed = bts['trial_start/data'][:]\n        teleport_flag = bts['teleport/data'][:]\n        environment = bts['environment/data'][:]\n        scanning = bts['scanning/data'][:]\n        pos_timestamps = bts['position/timestamps'][:]\n\n        # Reward: stored with separate timestamps\n        reward_timestamps = bts['Reward/timestamps'][:]\n        reward_data = bts['Reward/data'][:]\n\n        #
